  "target": "<think>\nSeen: couch in frame 13; telephone in frame 32; radiator in frame 6.\nIf I stood at the couch facing the telephone, the radiator would be to my left.\nThe question has me facing in the opposite direction of the telephone.   "target": "<think>\nSeen: refrigerator in frame 17; table in frame 31; door in frame 6.\nIf I stood at the refrigerator facing the table, the door would be to my right.\nThe question has me face away from the table. That is a half turn: what was ahead is now behind me, what was behind is now ahead, and left and right swap.\nSo I find the door to my left.\n</think>\n\nB",
  "facing": "away",
  "pair_id": "vsi590k_041016",
  "source_qid": "vsi590k_041016",
  "source_set": "T1H1_gaps"
}

  "target": "<think>\nSeen: refrigerator in frame 24; tv in frame 6; dishwasher in frame 24.\nIf I stood at the refrigerator facing the tv, the dishwasher would be behind me, on my left.\nThe question has me facing in the opposite direction of the tv. That is a half turn: what was ahead is now behind me, what was behind is now ahead, and left and right swap.\nSo I find the dishwasher at my front-right.\n</think>\n\nB",
  "validation_commit": ""
}

  "target": "<think>\nSeen: door in frame 22; backpack in frame 15; trash can in frame 26.\nIf I stood at the door facing the backpack, the trash can would be in front of me, on my left.\nThe question has me face away from the backpack. That is a half turn: what was ahead is now behind me, what was behind is now ahead, and left and right swap.\nSo I find the trash can at my back-right.\n</think>\n\nC",
  "facing": "away",
  "pair_id": "vsi590k_001484",
  "source_qid": "vsi590k_001484",

}

  "target": "<think>\nSeen: whiteboard in frame 7; cup in frame 5; file folder in frame 28.\nIf I stood at the whiteboard facing the cup, the file folder would be ahead of me, slightly to my left.\nThe question has me facing in the opposite direction of the cup. That is a half turn: what was ahead is now behind me, what was behind is now ahead, and left and right swap.\nSo I find the file folder at my back.\n</think>\n\nC",

}

  "target": "<think>\nSeen: chair in frame 18; paper bag in frame 20; door in frame 25.\nIf I stood at the chair facing the paper bag, the door would be out to my right.\nThe question has me face away from the paper bag. That is a half turn: what was ahead is now behind me, what was behind is now ahead, and left and right swap.\nSo I find the door to my left.\n</think>\n\nC",
  "facing": "away",
  "pair_id": "vsi590k_131585",
  "source_qid": "vsi590k_131585",
